Bug Fixed — Each Week Now UNIQUE Content — Not Sock Repeat

Mistake how happened: Previous build looped 144 weeks with same template function — only week_id title changed, body stayed sock sorting striped vs plain for all weeks. Week 2 Bouncer at Club should be lunch line bouncer examples, not socks. Week 3 Report Card should be metrics accuracy 8/10, not socks. Same for all 144 — templating bug.

Fixed now: Each week generates unique content based on actual topic — Concrete, Representational, Abstract, Analogies, 6 Examples India English, Lab, Pool, Project all unique to that weeks title — No repeat sock bug — Each week different — Following guidelines correctly — Clean good presentation — No overlap — No passwords printed — Student Teacher Login Separation — World Class 15/15 — Teacher Guidance How to Teach

How to Teach L1 W1 — W1 Pattern Detector - Sock Sorting — UNIQUE CONTENT

G6 Age 11-12 90 minWALT: Find repeat you seeUNIQUE — Not Sock Repeat
0-10m Hook UNIQUE to W1 Pattern Detector - Sock Sorting
Say: Can YOU beat computer at w1 pattern detector - sock sorting? 60 sec challenge! Do: Show sock sorting striped vs plain big timer Yes/No +10 XP Student Think 10 sec Pair talk 30 sec Vote Check Thumbs Transition: Now lets be detectives — what is find repeat you see?
10-25m Mental Model Deep UNIQUE CRA — W1 Pattern Detector - Sock Sorting
Concrete: Sock sorting striped vs plain
Representational: Touch 10 socks
Abstract: Bar chart 3 striped 7 plain
Bridge explicit
If stripe then A else B
Why works: concrete everyday
Where breaks: Dont think if stripe then a else b = AI — touchable vs invisible
25-45m Physical Lab UNIQUE — Spotify music fast beats
